I always look for ways AI can make a real difference in day-to-day work, and this tool now helps our team and customers across multiple time zones save time and avoid scheduling headaches. My approach with AI tools is always focused on practical applications that improve productivity, and Manus made that process surprisingly easy and efficient.

If you&#x27;ve been curious about building with AI, Manus is a great way to start just pick a real problem and try it out.</div></div><div class="flex items-start gap-3 w-full"><img src="https://web.archive.org/web/20250507044416im_/https://files.manuscdn.com/webapp/_next/static/media/CarlosRomo.77f5f8a7.webp" class="w-[36px] h-[36px] rounded-full object-cover flex-shrink-0 1.5xl:w-[48px] 1.5xl:h-[48px] xl:w-[42px] xl:h-[42px]"/><div class="flex flex-col items-start flex-1"><div class="text-[var(--text-primary)] text-[14px] font-[600] leading-[24px] 1.5xl:text-lg xl:text-[16px]">Carlos Romo</div><div class="text-[var(--text-tertiary)] text-[12px] leading-[20px] 1.5xl:text-sm xl:text-[13px] 1.5xl:leading-[18px] xl:leading-[16px] max-md:line-clamp-2" title="Top-Level IT Staffing &amp;
